```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 target=n/2
 for _ in range(50):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
   if diff>0:
     mask=h<1-1e-15
     add=diff/mask.sum()
     if add >= 1-h[mask].max():? Need iterative clipping: h+=add; clip
   else:
     mask=h>1e-15
     sub=-diff/mask.sum()
     h-=sub; clip
 return h
```

```
def univariate_min_index(h, c, idx, current_max):
    n=len(h); tau=n-1
    m1=max(0, tau-idx); m2=min(2*tau, 2*tau-idx)
    m3=max(0, idx); m4=min(2*tau, idx+tau)
    ms=np.union1d(np.arange(m1,m2+1), np.arange(m3,m4+1)) # costly
```

Functions:
```
def _feasible_intervals(V, c_aff, a, diag_pos, lo_d, hi_d):
    n=len(c_aff)
    L=lo_d; U=hi_d
    if L>U: return []
    if n==0: return [(L,U)]
    mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
    if diag_pos is not None: mask[diag_pos]=False
    c = c_aff[mask]; aa=a[mask]
    if len(c)>0:
        eps=1e-13
        pos=aa > 1e-12
        if np.any(pos):
            b = (V - c[pos]) / aa[pos]
            U = min(U, float(np.min(b)))
        neg=aa < -1e-12
        if np.any(neg):
            b = (V - c[neg]) / aa[neg]
            L = max(L, float(np.max(b)))
        zero= ~(pos|neg)
        if np.any(zero):
            if np.max(c[zero]) > V + 1e-12:
                return []
    if L > U + 1e-12:
        return []
    intervals = [(L,U)]
    if diag_pos is not None:
        cd = float(c_aff[diag_pos]); ad=float(a[diag_pos])
        const = V - cd
        disc = ad*ad - 4.0*const
        if disc > 1e-14:
            sq=np.sqrt(disc)
            r1=(-ad-sq)*0.5; r2=(-ad+sq)*0.5
            new=[]
            for l,u in intervals:
                if u <= r1 + 1e-12:
                    if l < u: new.append((l,u))
                elif l >= r2 - 1e-12:
                    if l < u: new.append((l,u))
                else:
                    if l < r1:
                        nl=l; nu=min(u,r1)
                        if nl < nu: new.append((nl,nu))
                    if u > r2:
                        nl=max(l,r2); nu=u
                        if nl < nu: new.append((nl,nu))
            intervals=new
        # if disc <=0 all feasible (P>=0)
    res=[]
    for l,u in intervals:
       l=max(l, lo_d); u=min(u, hi_d)
       if u - l > 1e-15:
          res.append((l,u))
       elif abs(l-lo_d)<=... include boundary? We can include point as (l,l)
       if u - l <= 1e-15: if l<=hi_d and l>=lo_d: res.append((l,l)) simplified filter.
    return res
```
Need for disc<=0 but if const? If disc <= 0, P = d²+ad+const has discriminant <=0; leading positive => P≥0 for all d? If disc negative, min >0. If disc zero, min 0. Yes all feasible. Good.

```
def update_variable(h, c, idx, n, tau, current_max_global? not needed):

  affected = c[lo:hi+1]

  cur = max(max_aff, unaff)
  if cur <= unaff + 1e-13: return ...
  ms = np.arange(lo, hi+1)
```

```
def smooth_gradient(h,n,alpha,top_k):
 c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
 m=c.max()
 t=alpha*(c-m)
 # To avoid overflow, shift max
 mx=t.max()
 e=np.exp(t-mx)
 total=e.sum()
 if total < 1e-300: weights = np.zeros_like(c); weights[argmax]=1
 else: weights=e/total
 # select top K? Could simply use all weights if n small (c length <2000). Summing gradient over all m with nonzero weights. For c length <=2400, all loop maybe 2400 per gradient; 100 iter ->240k, fine. But weights exponentially small. We can skip weights <1e-10.
 grad=np.zeros(n)
 pos=np.arange(n)
 for m,w in zip(indices, weights): if w<1e-12: continue
   j=pos+m-tau; vf=(j>=0)&(j<n); grad[pos[vf]] += w*(1-h[j[vf]])
   i=pos+tau-m; vb=(i>=0)&(i<n); grad[pos[vb]] -= w*h[i[vb]]
 # scale dx
 grad *= 2.0/n
 return grad
```

```
def grad_polish(h,n,iters,alphas,top_k=80,deadline=None,rng=None):
 h=h.copy(); cur=compute_c5(h,n); best=h.copy(); best_c=cur
 lr=0.02
 for alpha in alphas:
   vlr=lr
   no_improve=0
   for it in range(iters):
     if deadline and time.time()>deadline: break
     g=smooth_gradient(h,n,alpha,top_k)
     # maybe renormalize gradient norm
     gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
     if gn < 1e-14: break
     # scale step to move vector by ~0.05? h step = lr*g/ (gn?) If lr fixed h scale. Use g already dx scaled ~0.001? Let's estimate dx=0.0033, grad entries up to 2*0.0033? Actually smooth_gradient multiplies dx, so entries ~0.006. Step lr=1 gives h change 0.006. Good. Use lr=1.0 base. We can set h_new = project_feasible(h - g, n) maybe. Search.
     # backtracking with normalization: step = eta * g / max(1, gn?); use eta=0.5? Simpler: g_scaled = g / (np.linalg.norm(g)+1e-12); h_new = project(h - step_size*g_scaled). step_size=0.05? This changes all entries ~0.05/norm? Not.
```
Need calibrate. Objective C derivative wrt h in box: dx*weighted derivatives. dx=2/n. For n=600, dx=0.0033. Derivatives of c wrt h are sums of up to 2 values 0-1, so ∂C/∂h_i ~ dx*O(1)=0.0033. Gradient vector entries similar. A unit step along normalized gradient of L2 norm maybe ~0.01, but h changes across many coords. If subtract normalized * 0.1, each coord changes 0.1/norm ~0.004. Good. Use direction = g / norm, step size 0.05 to 0.02. Or use raw g: each coord changes up to 0.003, maybe too small but safe. Use raw with multiplier 20 => 0.06. We can backtracking.

```
 g=smooth_gradient
 gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
 if gn<1e-15: break
 dirn = -g/gn
 eta=0.08
 for tries in range(8):
   hp=project_feasible(h + eta*dirn, n)
   cp=compute_c5(hp,n)
   if cp < cur - 1e-12:
      h=hp; cur=cp; eta=min(eta*1.1,0.25); no_improve=0; update best; break
   else:
      eta*=0.5
 else:
   no_improve+=1; if no_improve>20: break
```

Let's detail `optimize_candidate` top-level:
```
def optimize_candidate(h0, n, deadline, rng, cd_passes=80, grad_alphas=(1,3,10,30,100), grad_iters=80, final_cd_passes=80, reserve=10):
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h0,float), n)
    c = compute_c5(h,n)
    # first CD
    h,c = coord_cd_exact(h,n,max_passes=cd_passes, deadline=deadline - max(reserve, 20), rng=rng)
    # gradient
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem > 30:
      h,c = grad_polish(h,n,iters=grad_iters, alphas=grad_alphas, deadline=deadline-reserve, rng=rng)
    # final CD
    h,c = coord_cd_exact(h,n,max_passes=final_cd_passes, deadline=deadline-reserve, rng=rng)
    # try 1-h
    hf = project_feasible(1-h,n); cf=compute_c5(hf,n)
    if cf<c: h,c=hf,cf
    # maybe second short CD after flip
    h,c=coord_cd_exact(h,n,max_passes=20, deadline=deadline-reserve, rng=rng)
    return h,c
```
But if called multiple times, deadline is global end; reserve for subsequent candidates. Need decide in run.

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)

 for _ in range(100):

   if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
   if diff>0:
     mask=h<1-1e-12
     if not np.any(mask): break

     if step >= max(1-h[mask]).min? 
   h=np.clip(h + (diff/np.count_nonzero(mask)) * mask, 0,1)? If adding uniform to unclipped mask may push some >1; then clip and repeat.
```

Pseudo run:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 best_h=None; best_c=1e100; best_n=0
 # helper to update best? no closure. Write inline.
 # Candidate 0: parent
 try:
   parent=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n_parent=len(parent)
 except Exception:
   parent=None; n_parent=0
 if parent is not None:
   h=project_feasible(parent,n_parent); c=compute_c5(h,n_parent)
   if c<best_c: best_h=parent...; best_n=n_parent
   # heavy optimize parent: use 45% budget?
```

Let's refine CD exact code with precomputed line arrays and top-level functions.

Function signatures:
```
def feasible_bool(V, cpos, apos, cneg, aneg, czero, dc, da, lo_d, hi_d, unaff):
    if V < unaff - 1e-12: return False
    L = lo_d; U = hi_d
    if apos.size:
        b = (V - cpos) / apos
        ub = float(np.min(b))
        if ub < U: U = ub
    if aneg.size:
        b = (V - cneg) / aneg
        lb = float(np.max(b))
        if lb > L: L = lb
    if czero > V + 1e-12:
        return False
    if L > U + 1e-12:
        return False
    if dc is not None:
        const = V - dc
        disc = da*da - 4.0*const
        if disc > 1e-14:
            sq = np.sqrt(disc)
            r1 = -0.5*(da - sq) # (-da - sq)/2
            r2 = -0.5*(da + sq)
            # r1 <= r2? If da? Let's test da=1, sq=2: r1=(-1-2)/2=-1.5, r2=(-1+2)/2=0.5. Using -0.5*(da - sq)= -0.5*( -1)=0.5 (r2). Wrong. Use directly:
            r1 = (-da - sq)*0.5
            r2 = (-da + sq)*0.5
            if not (U <= r1 + 1e-12 or L >= r2 - 1e-12):
                if L >= r1 - 1e-12 and U <= r2 + 1e-12:
                    return False
    return True
```

Now `_update_variable` top-level:
```
def update_variable(h,c,idx,n,tau,bis_iters=28):
    h0=float(h[idx])
    if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-14:
       return 0.0, 0.0, False
    lo = tau - idx
    if idx < lo: lo = idx
    b1 = tau + n - 1 - idx
    b2 = idx + tau
    hi = b1 if b1 > b2 else b2
    unaff=0.0
    total = 2*n - 1
    if lo > 0:
        m = float(np.max(c[:lo]))
        if m > unaff: unaff = m
    if hi + 1 < total:
        m = float(np.max(c[hi+1:]))
        if m > unaff: unaff = m
    ms = np.arange(lo, hi+1)
    aff = c[ms]  # view
    max_aff = float(np.max(aff))
    cur = max(max_aff, unaff)
    if cur - unaff <= 1e-13:
        return 0.0, cur, False
    a = np.zeros(ms.shape, dtype=float)
    jf = idx + ms - tau
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    if np.any(vf): a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    jb = idx - ms + tau
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    diag_pos = None
    if tau >= lo and tau <= hi:
        diag_pos = tau - lo
    dc = None; da = None
    if diag_pos is not None:
        dc = float(aff[diag_pos]); da = float(a[diag_pos])
        line_mask = np.ones(ms.shape, dtype=bool); line_mask[diag_pos] = False
        c_line = aff[line_mask].copy() # need copy? not modified
        a_line = a[line_mask].copy()
    else:
        c_line = aff.copy()
        a_line = a.copy()
    pos = a_line > 1e-12
    neg = a_line < -1e-12
    cpos = c_line[pos] if np.any(pos) else np.empty(0)
    apos = a_line[pos] if np.any(pos) else np.empty(0)
    cneg = c_line[neg] if np.any(neg) else np.empty(0)
    aneg = a_line[neg] if np.any(neg) else np.empty(0)
    if np.any(~(pos|neg)): czero = float(np.max(c_line[~(pos|neg)]))
    else: czero = -1e300
    lo_d = -h0; hi_d = 1.0 - h0
    low = unaff
    high = cur
    if high - low <= 1e-14: return 0.0, cur, False
    # Optional lower bound from line maxima? no
    for _ in range(bis_iters):
        mid = 0.5*(low+high)
        if feasible_bool(mid, cpos,apos,cneg,aneg,czero,dc,da,lo_d,hi_d,unaff):
            high = mid
        else:
            low = mid
    V = high
    intervals = final_intervals(V, cpos,apos,cneg,aneg,czero,dc,da,lo_d,hi_d,unaff)
    if not intervals:
       # fallback try high? or no update
       return 0.0, cur, False
    best_d=0.0; best_val=cur
    # add 0? if h0? d=0 val=cur not better.
    pts=[]
    for l,u in intervals:
       pts.append(l); pts.append(u); pts.append(0.5*(l+u))
    # deduplicate maybe not needed
    for d in pts:
       if d < lo_d - 1e-13 or d > hi_d + 1e-13: continue
       d = float(np.clip(d, lo_d, hi_d))
       vals = aff + a*d
       if diag_pos is not None:
           vals[diag_pos] -= d*d
       val = float(np.max(vals))
       if unaff > val: val = unaff
       if val < best_val - 1e-14:
          best_val=val; best_d=d
    if best_val < cur - 1e-12 and abs(best_d) > 1e-13:
       new_h = h0 + best_d
       if 0.0 <= new_h <= 1.0:
          # update c
          upd = a*best_d
          if diag_pos is not None:
             upd[diag_pos] -= best_d*best_d
          c[lo:hi+1] += upd
          # clip small negative
          # c[lo:hi+1] = np.maximum(c[lo:hi+1],0.0) maybe modifies view. Use slice copy? np.maximum(c[lo:hi+1],0,out=c[lo:hi+1])
          np.maximum(c[lo:hi+1], 0.0, out=c[lo:hi+1], where? np.maximum has out? yes np.maximum(c[lo:hi+1],0.0,out=c[lo:hi+1])
          return best_d, best_val, True
    return 0.0, cur, False
```
Need be careful: `aff = c[ms]` view. Later `vals = aff + a*d` uses aff. If we update c after evaluating, okay. But if we call np.maximum on c after, okay.
Memory: c_line etc copies each variable. Could reuse? Fine.

```
def coord_cd_exact(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng,bis_iters=0):
   tau=n-1; best_h=project_feasible(h,n).copy(); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
   if bis_iters==0: bis_iters=28 if n<=800 else 24
   no_pass=0
   for p in range(max_passes):
      if time.time() >= deadline: break
      h_cur = best_h.copy()
      c = np.correlate(h_cur,1-h_cur,'full')
      updated=False
      order = rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
      local_max_est = float(c.max()) # not needed
      for idx in order:
         if time.time() >= deadline: break
         d, val, ok = update_variable(h_cur,c,int(idx),n,tau,bis_iters)
         if ok:
            h_cur[idx] += d
            updated=True
      if not updated: break
      hp = project_feasible(h_cur,n)
      cp = compute_c5(hp,n)
      if cp < best_c - 1e-12:
         best_h = hp; best_c = cp; no_pass=0
      else:
         no_pass += 1
         if no_pass >= 6: break
   return best_h,best_c
```
This recomputes c from best_h each pass. It does not use in-place c across passes? Within pass yes. But after pass, if accepted, best_h projected; c for next pass recomputed. Good.

But note: `update_variable` uses `c` that is being updated in place. It also uses `h_cur` that is updated. However, when it updates c, the `c` array values for lags affected by previous updates are current. Good. At pass start, c correlated with feasible h. After updates, c reflects infeasible h? Since c updated asymptotically linearly for changed variables, but after multiple variable changes, is c exactly equal to correlate(h_cur,1-h_cur)? We update each variable's effect based on prior h. Since c_m is bilinear; sequential updates with exact delta (linear for non-diag, quadratic for diag) should keep c exact (up to round) for the current set of updated variables, independent of sum. Let's verify: When updating variable i, c_new = old c + a_i(d)*d - diag d² where a_i computed from old h. If previous updates made c exact for old h, and only h_i changes, formula exact. So c remains exact for current h_cur. Good! Sum drift doesn't matter. So within pass c exact. Great.

Projection at end changes all h, c recompute. Good.

Temporal: `update_variable` performs bisection 28 with vector ops. For all n in pass, maybe CPU. If no updates after some passes, break.

```
def grad_polish(h,n,iters,alphas,deadline,rng,top_k=80,eta0=0.06):
  h=project_feasible(h,n).copy(); cur=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur
  if len(alphas)==0: return best_h,best_c
  it_per = max(1,int(iters/len(alphas)))
  for alpha in alphas:
    eta = eta0
    stall = 0
    for it in range(it_per):
      if time.time() >= deadline: return best_h,best_c
      g = smooth_gradient(h,n,alpha,top_k)
      gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
      if gn < 1e-13: break
      dirn = g/gn
      step = eta
      accepted = False
      for trial in range(6):
         if time.time() >= deadline: break
         hp = project_feasible(h - step*dirn, n)
         cp = compute_c5(hp,n)
         if cp < cur - 1e-12:
            h=hp; cur=cp; step=min(step*1.05,0.25); accepted=True
            if cur < best_c - 1e-13: best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur
            break
         step *= 0.5
      if accepted:
         stall=0
      else:
         stall += 1
         eta = max(step, eta*0.6)
         if stall > 25: break
     # optionally random jumps if stall? maybe no.
  return best_h,best_c
```
Maybe after no accept for many, break all? Good.

But if gradient direction not improving due projection, step shrinks. If no accept 25, break alpha.

```
def make_thue(n):
    t=np.array([0.0])
    while t.size < n:
       t=np.concatenate((t, 1.0-t))
    t=t[:n]
    # ensure enough ones? project handles. Could flip to have ~half.
    return t
```

```
def try_rolls(h,n,rng,deadline,n_tries=12):
  h=project_feasible(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
  # deterministic shifts
  shifts=[0]*? cannot list >32? We can generate set.
  candidates=[]
  candidates.extend([n//4, n//2, n//3, 2*n//5, 3*n//7])
  if rng is not None:
     for _ in range(n_tries): candidates.append(int(rng.randint(n)))
  seen=set()
  for s in candidates:
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    s=s%n
    if s in seen: continue
    seen.add(s)
    hr=np.roll(h,s)
    cr=compute_c5(hr,n)
    if cr < best_c - 1e-12:
       best_h=hr; best_c=cr
  return best_h,best_c
```

`pair_search` time loop:
```
def pair_search(h0,n,deadline,rng,max_iters=2000,bis_iters=26,use_grad=True,grad_every=3):
 tau=n-1
 h=project_feasible(h0,n).copy()
 c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
 best_h=h.copy(); best_c_count=float(c.max())
 stall=0
 for it in range(max_iters):
   if time.time() >= deadline: break
   cur = best_c_count  # if c exact max should equal; but after moves c exact. Could compute if worried.
   # choose pair
   if use_grad and it % grad_every == 0:
      mstar=int(np.argmax(c))
      g=gradient_one_lag(h,c? mstar,n) # no need c
      i=-1;j=-1
      mask_inc=h<1-1e-8; mask_dec=h>1e-8
      if np.any(mask_inc) and np.any(mask_dec):
         i=argmin_mask(g,mask_inc); j=argmax_mask(g,mask_dec)
      if i==-1 or j==-1 or i==j:
         # try reverse: i=max, j=min? maybe
         i=argmax_mask(g,mask_inc); j=argmin_mask(g,mask_dec)
      if i==-1 or j==-1 or i==j:
         # random
         i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n-1)); ...
   else:
      # random pair with bias: choose i from zeros/low, j from ones/high?
      # If h has fractions, choose random distinct.
      i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n-1)); if j>=i:j+=1
   d,val,ok = update_pair(h,c,i,j,n,tau,bis_iters,apply=False)
   if ok and val < best_c_count - 1e-10: # or < cur?
      # apply by calling update_pair again apply=True? It may choose same best; to avoid recompute, update manually? We don't have b. Call again.
      d2,val2,ok2 = update_pair(h,c,i,j,n,tau,bis_iters,apply=True)
      if ok2:
         h[i] += d2; h[j] - d2; # wait update_pair also? It should not update h, only c? In earlier design update_pair updates c but not h. In single update, we updated h outside. Let's keep update_pair not modify h, only c if apply. Then pair_search updates h. For apply=True call returns d used. But update_pair apply=True modifies c. We can use d2.
         best_c_count=val2; best_h=h.copy()
         stall=0
   else:
      stall+=1
      if stall>200: break
 h=project_feasible(h,n); cp=compute_c5(h,n); return h,cp
```
But if we call update_pair apply=False then apply=True, we recompute and maybe due floating same. Overhead double for accepted only. Fine.

Need `argmin_mask` top-level:
```
def argmin_mask(arr, mask):
  if not np.any(mask): return -1
  masked = np.where(mask, arr, 1e300)
  return int(np.argmin(masked))
```

```
import numpy as np, time

def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * (2.0/n))

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target = n/2.0
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
       if diff > 0:
          if float(h.max()) + diff <= 1.0 + 1e-14:
             h += diff
          else:
             lo=0.0; hi=1.0-float(h.min())
             for _ in range(35):
                mid=0.5*(lo+hi); s=np.clip(h+mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
                if s < target: lo=mid
                else: hi=mid
             h=np.clip(h+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
       else:
          if float(h.min()) + diff >= -1e-14:
             h += diff
          else:
             lo=-float(h.max()); hi=0.0
             for _ in range(35):
                mid=0.5*(lo+hi); s=np.clip(h+mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
                if s < target: lo=mid
                else: hi=mid
             h=np.clip(h+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    res = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(res) > 1e-13:
       for _ in range(n):
          res = target - float(h.sum())
          if abs(res) <= 1e-14: break
          if res > 0:
             idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h)); add=min(res,1.0-h[idx])
             if add <= 0: break
             h[idx] += add
          else:
             idx=int(np.argmin(h)); sub=min(-res,h[idx])
             if sub <= 0: break
             h[idx] -= sub
    return h
```
Line count ~30.

`feasible_bool`:
```
def feasible_bool(V, cp, ap, cn, an, cz, dc, db, dk, lo_d, hi_d, unaff):
    if V < unaff - 1e-12: return False
    L=lo_d; U=hi_d
    if ap.size:
       U=min(U, float(np.min((V-cp)/ap)))
    if an.size:
       L=max(L, float(np.max((V-cn)/an)))
    if cz > V + 1e-12: return False
    if L > U + 1e-12: return False
    if dk > 0:
       disc=db*db - 4.0*dk*(V-dc)
       if disc > 1e-14:
          sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(-db-sq)/(2.0*dk); r2=(-db+sq)/(2.0*dk)
          if not (U <= r1 + 1e-12 or L >= r2 - 1e-12):
             if L >= r1 - 1e-12 and U <= r2 + 1e-12:
                return False
    return True
```
~17.

`final_intervals`:
```
def final_intervals(V, cp, ap, cn, an, cz, dc, db, dk, lo_d, hi_d, unaff):
   if V < unaff - 1e-12: return []
   L=lo_d; U=hi_d
   if ap.size: U=min(U,float(np.min((V-cp)/ap)))
   if an.size: L=max(L,float(np.max((V-cn)/an)))
   if cz > V + 1e-12: return []
   if L > U + 1e-12: return []
   iv=[(L,U)]
   if dk>0:
      disc=db*db - 4.0*dk*(V-dc)
      if disc > 1e-14:
         sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(-db-sq)/(2.0*dk); r2=(-db+sq)/(2.0*dk)
         nv=[]
         for l,u in iv:
            if u <= r1+1e-12:
               if u-l>1e-14: nv.append((l,u))
            elif l >= r2-1e-12:
               if u-l>1e-14: nv.append((l,u))
            else:
               if l < r1:
                  nl=l; nu=min(u,r1)
                  if nu-nl>1e-14: nv.append((nl,nu))
               if u > r2:
                  nl=max(l,r2); nu=u
                  if nu-nl>1e-14: nv.append((nl,nu))
         iv=nv
   out=[]
   for l,u in iv:
      l=max(l,lo_d); u=min(u,hi_d)
      if u-l > 1e-14:
         out.append((l,u))
      elif l >= lo_d-1e-14 and l <= hi_d+1e-14:
         out.append((l,l))
   return out
```
~35.

`update_variable` and `update_pair` share line setup. Could write helper `prepare_line_arrays(aff, a, diag_pos)` top-level to reduce duplication:
```
def line_arrays(aff, a, diag_pos):
    if diag_pos is not None:
       mask=np.ones(aff.shape,bool); mask[diag_pos]=False
       c_line=aff[mask].copy(); a_line=a[mask].copy()
    else:
       c_line=aff.copy(); a_line=a.copy()
    pos=a_line > 1e-12; neg=a_line < -1e-12
    cp=c_line[pos] if pos.any() else np.empty(0)
    ap=a_line[pos] if pos.any() else np.empty(0)
    cn=c_line[neg] if neg.any() else np.empty(0)
    an=a_line[neg] if neg.any() else np.empty(0)
    cz=float(np.max(c_line[~pos&~neg])) if np.any(~pos&~neg) else -1e300
    return cp,ap,cn,an,cz
```

But `~pos&~neg` precedence? Use `zero = ~(pos | neg)`.

`update_variable`:
```
def update_variable(h,c,idx,n,tau,bis):
    h0=float(h[idx])
    if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1.0-1e-14: return 0.0,0.0,False
    l1=tau-idx; r1=tau+n-1-idx; l2=idx; r2=idx+tau
    lo=min(l1,l2); hi=max(r1,r2)
    total=2*n-1; unaff=0.0
    if lo>0:
       m=float(np.max(c[:lo])); unaff=unaff if unaff>m else m
    if hi+1<total:
       m=float(np.max(c[hi+1:])); unaff=unaff if unaff>m else m
    ms=np.arange(lo,hi+1); aff=c[ms]
    cur_aff=float(np.max(aff)); cur=max(cur_aff,unaff)
    if cur-unaff <= 1e-13: return 0.0,cur,False
    a=np.zeros(ms.shape,float)
    jf=idx+ms-tau; vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n); a[vf]+=1.0-h[jf[vf]]
    jb=idx-ms+tau; vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n); a[vb]-=h[jb[vb]]
    diag=None
    if lo <= tau <= hi: diag=tau-lo
    dc=db=0.0; dk=0.0
    if diag is not None:
       dc=float(aff[diag]); db=float(a[diag]); dk=1.0
    cp,ap,cn,an,cz=line_arrays(aff,a,diag)
    los=-h0; hij=1.0-h0
    low=unaff; high=cur
    if high-low <=1e-14: return 0.0,cur,False
    for _ in range(bis):
       mid=0.5*(low+high)
       if feasible_bool(mid, cp,ap,cn,an, cz,dc,db,dk, los,hij,unaff): high=mid
       else: low=mid
    V=high
    iv=final_intervals(V, cp,ap,cn,an, cz,dc,db,dk, los,hij,unaff)
    if not iv: return 0.0,cur,False
    bd=0.0; bv=cur
    for l,u in iv:
       for dd in (l,u,0.5*(l+u)):
          if dd<los-1e-13 or dd>hij+1e-13: continue
          dd=float(np.clip(dd,los,hij))
          vals=aff+a*dd
          if dk>0: vals[diag]-=dk*dd*dd
          val=float(np.max(vals));
          if unaff>val: val=unaff
          if val<bv-1e-14: bv=val; bd=dd
    if bv < cur - 1e-12 and abs(bd)>1e-13:
       nh=h0+bd
       if 0<=nh<=1:
          upd=a*bd
          if dk>0: upd[diag]-=dk*bd*bd
          c[lo:hi+1]+=upd
          np.maximum(c[lo:hi+1],0.0,out=c[lo:hi+1])
          return bd,bv,True
    return 0.0,cur,False
```
~50.

`coord_cd_exact` as above. It calls update_variable. Use bis_iters parameter default maybe based n.
```
def coord_cd_exact(h0,n,max_passes,deadline,rng,bis=None):
   if bis is None: bis=26 if n<=900 else 22
   h=project_feasible(h0,n); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   tau=n-1; no=0
   for p in range(max_passes):
      if time.time()>=deadline: break
      c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); updated=False
      order=rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
      for idx in order:
         if time.time()>=deadline: break
         d,val,ok=update_variable(h,c,int(idx),n,tau,bis)
         if ok:
            h[int(idx)] += d; updated=True
      if not updated: break
      hp=project_feasible(h,n); cp=compute_c5(hp,n)
      if cp < best_c - 1e-12:
         h=hp; best_c=cp; no=0
      else:
         no +=1
         if no >= 6: break
   return h,best_c
```
Could update h[idx] to np.clip? d within bounds but round. use `h[idx]=float(np.clip(h[idx]+d,0,1))`. Good.

`pair_search`:
```
def gradient_one_lag(h,n,m):
   tau=n-1; pos=np.arange(n); g=np.zeros(n)
   j=pos+m-tau; vf=(j>=0)&(j<n); g[pos[vf]] += 1.0 - h[j[vf]]
   ip=pos+tau-m; vb=(ip>=0)&(ip<n); g[pos[vb]] -= h[ip[vb]]
   return g

def argmin_mask(g,mask):
   if not np.any(mask): return -1
   masked=g.copy(); masked[~mask]=1e300
   return int(np.argmin(masked))
```
No lambdas.

```
def pair_search(h0,n,deadline,rng,max_iters=2000,bis=None,use_grad=True):
   if bis is None: bis=24 if n<=900 else 20
   tau=n-1
   h=project_feasible(h0,n)
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   best_h=h.copy(); best_count=float(c.max())
   stall=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.time()>=deadline: break
      cur=float(c.max())
      if use_grad and (it % 3 == 0 or stall > 20):
         m=int(np.argmax(c))
         g=gradient_one_lag(h,n,m)
         inc = h < 1.0 - 1e-9; dec = h > 1e-9
         i = argmin_mask(g,inc); j = argmax_mask(g,dec)
         if i == -1 or j == -1 or i == j:
            i2=argmax_mask(g,inc); j2=argmin_mask(g,dec)
            if i2 != -1 and j2 != -1 and i2 != j2:
               i,j=i2,j2
      else:
         i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n-1))
         if j>=i: j+=1
      if i==-1 or j==-1 or i==j:
         # random fallback
         i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n-1));
         if j>=i: j+=1
      d,val,ok=update_pair(h,c,i,j,n,tau,bis,apply=False)
      if ok and val < min(cur,best_count) - 1e-10:
         d2,val2,ok2=update_pair(h,c,i,j,n,tau,bis,apply=True)
         if ok2:
            h[i]=float(np.clip(h[i]+d2,0,1)); h[j]=float(np.clip(h[j]-d2,0,1))
            # recompute cur should val2, but due round maybe
            # if val2 < best_count: best_count=val2
            if val2 < best_count:
               best_count=val2; best_h=h.copy()
               stall=0
            else:
               # still accepted local but not global? set cur? but best unchanged? h changed, c updated.
               cur=val2
               # Should not happen if val2<best? Maybe if best_count stale. Update if val2<best_count.
               if val2 < best_count: best_count=val2; best_h=h.copy()
               stall=0
         else:
            stall+=1
      else:
         stall+=1
         if stall > 300: break
   hp=project_feasible(h,n)
   cp=compute_c5(hp,n)
   if cp < best_count*(2.0/n) - 1e-12:?
      return hp,cp
   # We should return feasible hp and best_h? If hp worse due round, return best_h?
   # Since h feasible and sum preserved, projection shouldn't worsen much. But to be safe track best feasible.
```

```
def try_rolls(h,n,rng,deadline,tries=10):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   base_shifts=[0, n//4, n//2, n//3, 2*n//5, 3*n//7, n//8, 3*n//8]
   for s in base_shifts + maybe random:

      s%=n; hr=np.roll(h,s); cr=compute_c5(hr,n)

   if rng: for _ in range(tries): s=int(rng.randint(n)); ...
   return best_h,best_c
```

`make_thue`, `make_sin`, `make_two_intervals` top-level concise.
```
def make_thue(n):
    t=np.array([0.0])
    while t.size<n: t=np.concatenate((t,1.0-t))
    return t[:n]

def make_sin(n,freq=1.0,phase=0.0):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
    return 0.5+0.5*np.sin(np.pi*freq*x+phase)

def make_two(n,a=None):
    h=np.zeros(n); half=n//2
    if a is None or a<=0 or a>half: a=half//2
    b=half-a
    h[:a]=1.0
    if b: h[a:a+b]=1.0
    return h
```
Line okay.

```
def optimize_candidate(h0,n,stage_deadline,rng,cd1=120,pair1=800,cd2=60,pair2=500, use_pair=True):
    t0=time.time(); slen=max(1.0, stage_deadline-t0)
    h=project_feasible(h0,n); c=compute_c5(h,n)
    if slen < 20: return h,c
    d1=t0+0.38*slen
    h,c=coord_cd_exact(h,n,cd1,d1,rng)
    d2=t0+0.58*slen
    if use_pair and time.time()<d2:
       h,c=pair_search(h,n,d2,rng,max_iters=pair1,use_grad=True)

    # maybe smooth? skip

    # flip

       h=hf; c=cf; h,c=coord_cd_exact(h,n,20,stage_deadline,rng)
    return h,c
```

Run schedule:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); B=budget_s; deadline=t0+B-5
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 best_h=None; best_c=1e100; best_n=0
 def? no closures, inline update.
 # parent or random
 try:
   parent=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n0=len(parent)
 except Exception:
   parent=None; n0=0
 if parent is None or n0<2:
    n0=600; parent=make_random? need a random. Use make_thue(600) maybe.
 # optimize parent
 t_parent=t0+0.35*B
 h,c=optimize_candidate(parent,n0,t_parent,rng,cd1=150,pair1=1200,cd2=80,pair2=800)
 if c<best_c: ...
 # try flip? optimize already
 # resolution candidates from best
 # choose list of (n, end_frac)
 res_list=[(800,0.52),(1000,0.66),(1200,0.78),(500,0.86)]
 for target_n,end_frac in res_list:
    if best_h is None: break
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem < 60: break
    if target_n == best_n: continue
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,best_n,endpoint=False); x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,target_n,endpoint=False)
    h_new=project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,best_h),target_n)
    stage_deadline=min(deadline, t0+end_frac*B)
    if stage_deadline <= time.time(): continue
    h,c=optimize_candidate(h_new,target_n,stage_deadline,rng,cd1=80,pair1=600,cd2=50,pair2=400)
    if c < best_c - 1e-12 or (abs(c-best_c)<=1e-12 and target_n<best_n): update
 # alternative seeds
 if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 80:
    alt_n=best_n if best_n>=500 and best_n<=1200 else (800 if best_n>1200 else 600)
    # split among 3 seeds fractions: 0.90,0.94,0.97
    seeds = [make_thue, make_two, random] but no closures. Loop with types.
    # Need stage deadlines: after res_list, use actual t0 + ...
    stage_fractions=[0.91,0.95,0.98]
    seed_types=[0,1,2]
    for typ,frac in zip(seed_types,stage_fractions):
       if time.time() >= min(deadline, t0+frac*B): continue
       n_alt=alt_n
       if typ==0: h_s=make_thue(n_alt)
       elif typ==1: h_s=make_two(n_alt, n_alt//5)
       else: h_s=np.zeros(n_alt); idx=rng.choice(n_alt,size=n_alt//2,replace=False); h_s[idx]=1; h_s=project_feasible(h_s,n_alt)
       h,c=optimize_candidate(h_s,n_alt,t0+frac*B,rng,cd1=50,pair1=300,cd2=30,pair2=200)
       if c<best_c: update
 # final roll and polish
 if best_h is not None:
    # roll search with deadline deadline-40
    h,c=try_rolls(best_h,best_n,rng,deadline-40,12)
    if c<best_c: update
    # final CD/pair short on best with remaining
    h,c=pair_search(best_h,best_n,deadline-20,rng,max_iters=800)
    if c<best_c: update
    h,c=coord_cd_exact(h,best_n,100,deadline-5,rng)
    if c<best_c: update
    # try flip and roll again?
    hf=project_feasible(1-bbest_n); cf=compute_c5...
    if cf<best_c: best=...; final pair short
 # final project and compute
 best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
 return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

```
def quadratic_feasible_intervals(q, c, b, V, lo, hi):
    if lo > hi: return []
    if q > 0:
       disc = b*b - 4.0*q*(c - V)
       if disc < -1e-13: return []
       if disc <= 1e-13:
          r = -b/(2.0*q)
          if r < lo - 1e-13 or r > hi + 1e-13: return []
          r = float(np.clip(r, lo, hi)); return [(r,r)]
       sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(-b-sq)/(2.0*q); r2=(-b+sq)/(2.0*q)
       l=max(lo,r1); u=min(hi,r2)
       if u-l > 1e-14: return [(l,u)]
       if l <= hi and u >= lo: l=max(lo,min(l,r?)) return [(clip)]? For point root? If disc>0 and interval zero length maybe not.
       return [(l,u)] if u>=l-eps else []
    else:

       disc = b*b - 4.0*k*(V - c)
       if disc <= 1e-13:
          return [(lo,hi)]
       sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(-b-sq)/(2.0*k); r2=(-b+sq)/(2.0*k)
       out=[]
       if lo < r1 or hi <= r1? Let's do generic intersection with [lo,hi] and outside:
          if hi <= r1: out [(lo,hi)]
          elif lo >= r2: out [(lo,hi)]
```

Implement `prepare_constraint` compact:
```
def prepare_constraint(aff, coeff, nonaff_list):
    if nonaff_list:
        mask = np.ones(aff.shape, dtype=bool)
        for loc, q in nonaff_list:
            mask[loc] = False
        cl = aff[mask].copy()
        al = coeff[mask].copy()
    else:
        cl = aff.copy(); al = coeff.copy()
    pos = al > 1e-12; neg = al < -1e-12
    cp = cl[pos] if pos.any() else np.empty(0)
    ap = al[pos] if pos.any() else np.empty(0)
    cn = cl[neg] if neg.any() else np.empty(0)
    an = al[neg] if neg.any() else np.empty(0)
    zero = ~(pos | neg)
    cz = float(np.max(cl[zero])) if zero.any() else -1e300
    data=[]
    for loc,q in nonaff_list:
       data.append((float(q), float(aff[loc]), float(coeff[loc])))
    return cp,ap,cn,an,cz,data
```
Note `~(pos | neg)` okay. If no nonaff, data empty.

`quadratic_feasible` and `intersect_intervals`. Then `feasible_generic` and `final_generic`.

```
def quadratic_feasible(q,c,b,V,lo,hi):
    if lo > hi: return []
    if q > 0.0:
       disc = b*b - 4.0*q*(c - V)
       if disc < -1e-13: return []
       if disc <= 1e-13:
          rr = -b/(2.0*q)
          if rr < lo - 1e-13 or rr > hi + 1e-13: return []
          rr = float(np.clip(rr, lo, hi))
          return [(rr, rr)]
       sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(-b-sq)/(2.0*q); r2=(-b+sq)/(2.0*q)
       l=max(lo,r1); u=min(hi,r2)
       if u-l > 1e-14: return [(l,u)]
       if u >= lo and l <= hi:
          mm=(l+u)*0.5; return [(mm,mm)]
       return []
    else:
       k=-q
       disc = b*b - 4.0*k*(V - c)
       if disc <= 1e-13:
          return [(lo,hi)]
       sq=np.sqrt(disc); r1=(-b-sq)/(2.0*k); r2=(-b+sq)/(2.0*k)
       out=[]
       if hi <= r1 + 1e-14:
          if hi-lo > 1e-14: out.append((lo,hi))
          else: out.append((lo,lo))
       elif lo >= r2 - 1e-14:
          if hi-lo > 1e-14: out.append((lo,hi))
          else: out.append((lo,lo))
       else:
          if lo < r1:
             nl=lo; nu=min(hi,r1)
             if nu-nl > 1e-14: out.append((nl,nu))
             else: out.append((nl,nl)) # endpoint feasible
          if hi > r2:
             nl=max(lo,r2); nu=hi
             if nu-nl > 1e-14: out.append((nl,nu))
             else: out.append((nl,nl))
       return out
```
For q<0, endpoints at roots feasible (P=0). If interval [lo,hi] entirely inside gap [r1,r2], no out. If lo<r1 but r1<lo? no.
For q>0, if feasible interval narrow point, return point.

`intersect_intervals` should include points. Use tolerance 1e-14.
```
def intersect_intervals(A,B):
 out=[]
 for l1,u1 in A:
   for l2,u2 in B:
     l=l1 if l1>l2 else l2; u=u1 if u1<u2 else u2
     if u-l > 1e-14: out.append((l,u))
     elif abs(u-l) <= 1e-14:
        pp=(l+u)*0.5; out.append((pp,pp))
 return out
```
Could have duplicates; small.
Maybe we want no point duplicates; okay.

```
def feasible_generic(V,cp,ap,cn,an,cz,data,lo_d,hi_d):

    if not iv: return False
 return True
```

`pair_search` logic details:
```
def pair_search(h0,n,deadline,rng,max_iters=2000,bis=None,use_grad=True):
    if bis is None: bis=22 if n<=900 else 18
    tau=n-1
    h=project_feasible(h0,n).copy()
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    best_h=h.copy(); best_count=float(c.max()); best_c=best_count*2.0/n
    stall=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.time()>=deadline: break
        cur=float(c.max())
        # selection
        if use_grad and (it % 4 == 0 or stall > 20):
            m=int(np.argmax(c))
            g=gradient_one_lag(h,n,m)
            inc=h < 1.0 - 1e-9; dec=h > 1e-9
            i=argmin_mask(g,inc); j=argmax_mask(g,dec)
            if i == -1 or j == -1 or i == j:
                i=argmax_mask(g,inc); j=argmin_mask(g,dec)
        if i == -1 or j == -1 or i == j:
            i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n-1));
            if j>=i: j+=1
        d,val,ok=update_pair(h,c,i,j,n,tau,bis,apply=False)
        if ok and val < min(cur,best_count) - 1e-10:
            d2,val2,ok2=update_pair(h,c,i,j,n,tau,bis,apply=True)
            if ok2:
                h[i]=float(np.clip(h[i]+d2,0,1)); h[j]=float(np.clip(h[j]-d2,0,1))
                if val2 < best_count - 1e-12:
                    best_count=val2; best_c=val2*2.0/n; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
                else:
                    # local improved but not global? still okay reset stall maybe
                    stall=0
            else:
                stall += 1
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 400: break
    hp=project_feasible(h,n); cp=compute_c5(hp,n)
    if cp < best_c - 1e-13:
       return hp,cp
    return best_h,best_c
```
But if accepted move improves cur but val2 not < best_count due rounding (e.g., best_count slightly lower due earlier but cur > best_count? shouldn't), we still updated h/c to a state maybe not best. Could hurt subsequent. We should track best feasible separately and if current h/c not best? But c exact and h feasible. If it didn't improve best_count, it might still be a different local with same count? Could continue. At end return best_h. But c/h current may differ from best_h; if best_h not current, future moves from a non-best may not improve best. To be safe, after accepted but not best, we can revert? We don't have old c. Simpler: Accept only if `val < best_count - 1e-12`, not cur. Since cur should equal best_count if we maintain, it's same. If not, maybe maintain `cur=best_count` by setting after each accepted? Let's ensure:
- Start cur=best_count.
- On accepted and val2 < best_count: update best_count=val2, current c max should be val2. Set cur=val2.
- If val2 not < best_count (shouldn't because condition val<best_count), revert? We can avoid by condition val < best_count - tol. Since ok val < cur and cur should equal best_count. If due floating, condition false and we never call apply. So no issue.
Thus in code check `if ok and val < best_count - 1e-10:` before apply. Drop min(cur,best). But cur might be slightly > best_count if c drift? We recompute cur each loop and update best_count when accept; c exact so cur=best_count. Good. Use `best_count`.
After apply, set `best_count = val2` and `best_h = h.copy()`. Also `cur = val2` for next loop (can just recompute next loop).
If no accept, h unchanged. Good.
At end return best_h.

```
   bases=[h, h[::-1], 1.0-h, 1.0-h[::-1]]
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0.0:
            if float(h.max()) + diff <= 1.0 + 1e-14:
                h = h + diff
            else:
                lo = 0.0
                hi = 1.0 - float(h.min())
                for _ in range(35):
                    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
                    s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
                    if s < target:
                        lo = mid
                    else:
                        hi = mid
                h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            if float(h.min()) + diff >= -1e-14:
                h = h + diff
            else:
                lo = -float(h.max())
                hi = 0.0
                for _ in range(35):
                    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
                    s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
                    if s < target:
                        lo = mid
                    else:
                        hi = mid
                h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    res = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(res) > 1e-13:
        for _ in range(n):
            res = target - float(h.sum())
            if abs(res) <= 1e-14:
                break
            if res > 0.0:
                idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
                add = min(res, 1.0 - h[idx])
                if add <= 1e-18:
                    break
                h[idx] += add
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmin(h))
                sub = min(-res, h[idx])
                if sub <= 1e-18:
                    break
                h[idx] -= sub
    return h
```
This is long but okay.

`quadratic_feasible`:
```
def quadratic_feasible(q,c,b,V,lo,hi):
    if lo > hi:
        return []
    if q > 0.0:
        disc = b*b - 4.0*q*(c - V)
        if disc < -1e-12:
            return []
        if disc <= 1e-12:
            rr = -b / (2.0*q)
            if rr < lo - 1e-12 or rr > hi + 1e-12:
                return []
            rr = float(np.clip(rr, lo, hi))
            return [(rr, rr)]
        sq = float(np.sqrt(disc))
        r1 = (-b - sq) / (2.0*q)
        r2 = (-b + sq) / (2.0*q)
        l = lo if lo > r1 else r1
        u = hi if hi < r2 else r2
        if u - l > 1e-13:
            return [(l, u)]
        if u >= lo and l <= hi:
            mm = 0.5 * (l + u)
            return [(mm, mm)]
        return []
    k = -q
    disc = b*b - 4.0*k*(V - c)
    if disc <= 1e-12:
        return [(lo, hi)]
    sq = float(np.sqrt(disc))
    r1 = (-b - sq) / (2.0*k)
    r2 = (-b + sq) / (2.0*k)
    out = []
    if hi <= r1 + 1e-12:
        return [(lo, hi)]
    if lo >= r2 - 1e-12:
        return [(lo, hi)]
    if lo < r1:
        nu = hi if hi < r1 else r1
        if nu - lo >= -1e-12:
            if nu - lo > 1e-13:
                out.append((lo, nu))
            else:
                out.append((lo, lo))
    if hi > r2:
        nl = lo if r2 < lo else r2
        if hi - nl >= -1e-12:
            if hi - nl > 1e-13:
                out.append((nl, hi))
            else:
                out.append((hi, hi))
    return out
```
In q<0, if interval [lo,hi] all feasible and point, return [(lo,hi)] even if point. Good.
For q>0 point, okay.

`intervals_for_V`:
```
def intervals_for_V(V, cp, ap, cn, an, cz, data, lo_d, hi_d):
    L=lo_d; U=hi_d
    if ap.size:
        bnd = float(np.min((V - cp)/ap))
        if bnd < U: U = bnd
    if an.size:
        bnd = float(np.max((V - cn)/an))
        if bnd > L: L = bnd
    if cz > V + 1e-11:
        return []
    if L > U + 1e-11:
        return []
    if L < lo_d: L=lo_d
    if U > hi_d: U=hi_d
    if L > U + 1e-12:
        return []
    iv = [(L, U)]
    for q, c, b in data:
        new = []
        for l, u in iv:
            new.extend(quadratic_feasible(q, c, b, V, l, u))
        iv = new
        if not iv:
            return []
    out=[]
    for l,u in iv:
        if l < lo_d: l=lo_d
        if u > hi_d: u=hi_d
        if u - l > 1e-13:
            out.append((l,u))
        else:
            mm=0.5*(l+u)
            out.append((mm,mm))
    return out
```
If U becomes <lo_d, L may still lo_d, then L>U return. Good. If L>hi_d return. Good.

`prepare_constraint`:
```
def prepare_constraint(aff, coeff, nonaff):
    if nonaff:
        mask = np.ones(aff.shape, dtype=bool)
        for loc, q in nonaff:
            mask[int(loc)] = False
        cl = aff[mask].copy()
        al = coeff[mask].copy()
    else:
        cl = aff.copy()
        al = coeff.copy()
    pos = al > 1e-12
    neg = al < -1e-12
    cp = cl[pos] if pos.any() else np.empty(0)
    ap = al[pos] if pos.any() else np.empty(0)
    cn = cl[neg] if neg.any() else np.empty(0)
    an = al[neg] if neg.any() else np.empty(0)
    zero = ~(pos | neg)
    cz = float(np.max(cl[zero])) if zero.any() else -1e300
    data = []
    for loc, q in nonaff:
        loc = int(loc)
        data.append((float(q), float(aff[loc]), float(coeff[loc])))
    return cp, ap, cn, an, cz, data
```

`update_variable`:
```
def update_variable(h,c,idx,n,tau,bis,apply=True):
    h0=float(h[idx])
    if h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1.0-1e-13:
        return 0.0,0.0,False
    lo = tau - idx
    if idx < lo: lo = idx
    hi = tau + n - 1 - idx
    rb = idx + tau
    if rb > hi: hi = rb
    total=2*n-1
    unaff=0.0
    if lo>0:
        m=float(np.max(c[:lo])); 
        if m>unaff: unaff=m
    if hi+1<total:
        m=float(np.max(c[hi+1:]));
        if m>unaff: unaff=m
    ms=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
    aff=c[ms]
    maxa=float(np.max(aff))
    cur=max(maxa,unaff)
    if cur - unaff <= 1e-12:
        return 0.0,cur,False
    coeff=np.zeros(ms.shape,dtype=float)
    jf=idx+ms-tau
    vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n)
    if vf.any(): coeff[vf]+=1.0-h[jf[vf]]
    jb=idx-ms+tau
    vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    if vb.any(): coeff[vb]-=h[jb[vb]]
    nonaff=[]
    if lo <= tau <= hi:
        nonaff.append((tau-lo,-1.0))
    cp,ap,cn,an,cz,data=prepare_constraint(aff,coeff,nonaff)
    los=-h0; hij=1.0-h0
    if los > hij + 1e-13:
        return 0.0,cur,False
    low=unaff; high=cur
    if high - low <= 1e-13:
        return 0.0,cur,False
    for _ in range(bis):
        mid=0.5*(low+high)
        if intervals_for_V(mid,cp,ap,cn,an,cz,data,los,hij):
            high=mid
        else:
            low=mid
    iv=intervals_for_V(high,cp,ap,cn,an,cz,data,los,hij)
    if not iv:
        return 0.0,cur,False
    bd=0.0; bv=cur
    for l,u in iv:
        for dd in (l,u,0.5*(l+u)):
            if dd < los - 1e-12 or dd > hij + 1e-12:
                continue
            dd=float(np.clip(dd,los,hij))
            vals=aff+coeff*dd
            for loc,q in nonaff:
                vals[int(loc)] += q*dd*dd
            val=float(np.max(vals))
            if unaff>val: val=unaff
            if val < bv - 1e-13:
                bv=val; bd=dd
    if bv < cur - 1e-12 and abs(bd)>1e-13:
        nh=h0+bd
        if 0.0 <= nh <= 1.0:
            if apply:
                upd=coeff*bd
                for loc,q in nonaff:
                    upd[int(loc)] += q*bd*bd
                c[lo:hi+1] += upd
                np.maximum(c[lo:hi+1],0.0,out=c[lo:hi+1])
            return bd,bv,True
    return 0.0,cur,False
```
Need `aff` is view; when we do `vals=aff+coeff*dd`, okay. When updating c, `upd` separate. But after `c[lo:hi+1] += upd`, `aff` view changes, not used. Good.
Potential: `if cur - unaff <= 1e-12` skip; if cur is in counts, tolerance maybe 1e-12 good. For C improvement, okay.
`np.maximum(c[lo:hi+1],0.0,out=c[lo:hi+1])` syntax: yes. Could use `np.clip(c[lo:hi+1],0.0,None,out=c[lo:hi+1])` but `np.maximum` out works.

`update_pair`:
```
def update_pair(h,c,i,j,n,tau,bis,apply=True):
    if i==j:
        return 0.0,0.0,False
    lo_i = tau - i
    if i < lo_i: lo_i = i
    hi_i = tau + n - 1 - i
    rb = i + tau
    if rb > hi_i: hi_i = rb
    lo_j = tau - j
    if j < lo_j: lo_j = j
    hi_j = tau + n - 1 - j
    rb = j + tau
    if rb > hi_j: hi_j = rb
    lo = lo_i if lo_i < lo_j else lo_j
    hi = hi_i if hi_i > hi_j else hi_j
    total=2*n-1
    unaff=0.0
    if lo>0:
       m=float(np.max(c[:lo])); ...
    if hi+1<total: ...
    ms=np.arange(lo,hi+1); M=ms.size; aff=c[ms]
    cur_aff=float(np.max(aff)); cur=max(cur_aff,unaff)
    if cur-unaff <=1e-12: return
    coeff=np.zeros(M,float)
    # i +
    jf=i+ms-tau; vf=...; coeff[vf]+=1-h[jf[vf]]
    jb=i-ms+tau; vb=...; coeff[vb]-=h[jb[vb]]
    # j -
    jf=j+ms-tau; vf=...; coeff[vf]-=1-h[jf[vf]]
    jb=j-ms+tau; vb=...; coeff[vb]+=h[jb[vb]]
    nonaff=[(tau-lo,-2.0)]
    m1=tau+(j-i)
    if 0 <= m1 < total:
       loc=m1-lo
       if 0 <= loc < M and not any(l==loc for l,_ in nonaff): nonaff.append((loc,1.0))
    m2=tau+(i-j)
    if 0 <= m2 < total:
       loc=m2-lo
       if 0 <= loc < M and not any(l==loc for l,_ in nonaff): nonaff.append((loc,1.0))
    cp,ap,cn,an,cz,data=prepare_constraint(aff,coeff,nonaff)
    h0=float(h[i]); h1=float(h[j])
    los=max(-h0, h1-1.0); hij=min(1.0-h0, h1)
    if los > hij + 1e-13: return
    low=unaff; high=cur
    if high-low <=1e-13: return
    for _ in range(bis): ...
    iv=intervals_for_V(high,...)
    if not iv: return
    bd=0; bv=cur
    for l,u in iv: ... vals=aff+coeff*dd; for loc,q in nonaff: vals[loc]+=q*dd*dd
    accept: nh_i=h0+bd; nh_j=h1-bd; if within:
        if apply: upd=coeff*bd; for loc,q: upd[loc]+=q*bd*bd; c[lo:hi+1]+=upd; clip
        return bd,bv,True
```
Need `any(l==loc for l,_ in nonaff)` is a generator expression; does that count as non-lambda? It's a generator, not lambda/closure? It's allowed? The rules say no closures or lambdas. A generator expression technically creates a generator, maybe okay but could be considered closure? To be safe, avoid generator. Use simple loop:
```
exists=False
for existed,qq in nonaff:
  if existed==loc: exists=True
if not exists: nonaff.append(...)
```
Do for both.

Also in `prepare_constraint` `for loc, q in nonaff` okay (not closure).

In candidate eval, for each dd, `vals=aff+coeff*dd` creates array. For nonaff small loop. Good.

`coord_cd_exact` as above. Use default bis:
```
def coord_cd_exact(h0,n,max_passes,deadline,rng,bis=None):
    if n < 4:
        h=project_feasible(h0,n); return h, compute_c5(h,n)
    if bis is None:
        bis=24 if n<=700 else (20 if n<=1100 else 16)
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    tau=n-1; no=0
    for _ in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>=deadline: break
       c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
       updated=False
       order=rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
       for idx in order:
          if time.time()>=deadline: break
          d,val,ok=update_variable(h,c,int(idx),n,tau,bis,True)
          if ok:
             h[int(idx)]=float(np.clip(h[int(idx)]+d,0.0,1.0))
             updated=True
       if not updated: break
       hp=project_feasible(h,n)
       cp=compute_c5(hp,n)
       if cp < best_c - 1e-12:
          h=hp; best_c=cp; no=0
       else:
          no += 1
          if no >= 6: break
    return h,best_c
```
Potential: If `update_variable` applies c update but h not updated yet? We update h after. c update uses old h; after h update, c exact. Good.

`gradient_one_lag`:
```
def gradient_one_lag(h,n,m):
    tau=n-1
    pos=np.arange(n)
    g=np.zeros(n)
    j=pos+m-tau
    vf=(j>=0)&(j<n)
    if vf.any(): g[pos[vf]] += 1.0 - h[j[vf]]
    ip=pos+tau-m
    vb=(ip>=0)&(ip<n)
    if vb.any(): g[pos[vb]] -= h[ip[vb]]
    return g
```

`argmin_mask`, `argmax_mask`:
```
def argmin_mask(g,mask):
    if not mask.any(): return -1
    return int(np.argmin(np.where(mask,g,1e300)))
```
Argmax similar with -1e300.

`pair_search` as above. Use bis default based n. In selection, if n<2 return. Need ensure `rng.randint(n-1)` valid for n>1.
```
def pair_search(h0,n,deadline,rng,max_iters=2000,bis=None,use_grad=True):
    if n < 4:
        h=project_feasible(h0,n); return h,compute_c5(h,n)
    if bis is None:
        bis=22 if n<=700 else (18 if n<=1100 else 15)
    h=project_feasible(h0,n).copy()
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    best_h=h.copy(); best_count=float(c.max()); best_c=best_count*2.0/n
    if use_grad:
        inc = h < 1.0 - 1e-9
        dec = h > 1e-9
        # cannot precompute because h changes
    stall=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.time()>=deadline: break
        if use_grad and (it % 4 == 0 or stall > 20):
            m=int(np.argmax(c))
            g=gradient_one_lag(h,n,m)
            inc=h < 1.0 - 1e-9
            dec=h > 1e-9
            if inc.any() and dec.any():
                i=argmin_mask(g,inc); j=argmax_mask(g,dec)
                if i == -1 or j == -1 or i == j:
                    i=argmax_mask(g,inc); j=argmin_mask(g,dec)
            else:
                i=j=-1
        else:
            i=j=-1
        if i == -1 or j == -1 or i == j:
            i=int(rng.randint(n))
            j=int(rng.randint(n-1))
            if j >= i: j += 1
            if j >= n: j -= n
        d,val,ok=update_pair(h,c,i,j,n,tau,bis,apply=False)
        if ok and val < best_count - 1e-10:
            d2,val2,ok2=update_pair(h,c,i,j,n,tau,bis,apply=True)
            if ok2:
                h[i]=float(np.clip(h[i]+d2,0.0,1.0))
                h[j]=float(np.clip(h[j]-d2,0.0,1.0))
                if val2 < best_count - 1e-12:
                    best_count=val2
                    best_c=val2*2.0/n
                    best_h=h.copy()
                stall=0
            else:
                stall += 1
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 400:
                break
    hp=project_feasible(h,n)
    cp=compute_c5(hp,n)
    if cp < best_c - 1e-13:
        return hp,cp
    return best_h,best_c
```
Need `tau` defined: `tau=n-1`. Add.
Random j: `j=int(rng.randint(n-1)); if j>=i: j+=1; if j>=n: j-=n` ensures distinct. If n=2, j in {0}; if i=0 j=1; if i=1 j=0. Good.
Potential bug: `best_c` used in final if `best_count` updated. If no update, okay. If `val2` accepted but not < best_count? We condition val<best_count then apply; val2 should. If not, best_h not updated but h/c changed to maybe worse. But condition should prevent. Good.

`make_thue`:
```
def make_thue(n):
    t=np.array([0.0])
    while t.size < n:
        t=np.concatenate((t, 1.0 - t))
    return t[:n]
```
Line. This builds large by concatenation repeatedly; for n=1200 okay. But `t` int? float. Good.

`make_random_balanced`:
```
def make_random_balanced(n,rng):
    h=np.zeros(n)
    k=n//2
    if k:
        idx=rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
        h[idx]=1.0
    if n%2:
        h[rng.randint(n)] += 0.5
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
If n even. If n odd target k+0.5, add 0.5. Project handles. If k=0 n=1, okay.

`make_two`:
```
def make_two(n, a=None):
    h=np.zeros(n); half=n//2
    if a is None or a <=0 or a > half: a=half//2
    if a<0:a=0
    b=half-a
    h[:a]=1.0
    if b: h[a:a+b]=1.0
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

`symmetry_search` (rename `try_rolls` maybe). Use bases and shifts. Need avoid long literal; lists small.
```
def try_rolls(h0,n,rng,deadline,extra=4):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    bases=(h, h[::-1], 1.0-h, 1.0-h[::-1])
    shifts=(0, n//4, n//2, n//3, 2*n//5, 3*n//7)
    for b in bases:
        for s in shifts:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                return best_h, best_c
            hs=np.roll(b, int(s) % n)
            cs=compute_c5(hs,n)
            if cs < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_c=cs; best_h=hs.copy()
    if rng is not None:
        for _ in range(extra):
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            s=int(rng.randint(n))
            hs=np.roll(best_h, s)
            cs=compute_c5(hs,n)
            if cs < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_c=cs; best_h=hs.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```
Bases tuple length4 okay, shifts length6. If n maybe 0? n>0. `1.0-h` no project needed. Good.

`optimize_candidate`:
```
def optimize_candidate(h0,n,stage_deadline,rng,cd1=100,pair1=600,cd2=60,pair2=400):
    st=time.time(); slen=max(1.0, stage_deadline-st)
    h=project_feasible(h0,n); c=compute_c5(h,n)
    if slen < 30:
        return h,c
    d1=st+0.35*slen
    h,c=coord_cd_exact(h,n,cd1,d1,rng)
    d2=st+0.60*slen
    if time.time() < d2:
        h,c=pair_search(h,n,d2,rng,max_iters=pair1)
    d3=st+0.80*slen
    h,c=coord_cd_exact(h,n,cd2,d3,rng)
    d4=st+0.90*slen
    if time.time() < d4:
        h,c=pair_search(h,n,d4,rng,max_iters=pair2)
    hf=project_feasible(1.0-h,n)
    cf=compute_c5(hf,n)
    if cf < c - 1e-12 and time.time() < stage_deadline - 5:
        h,c=coord_cd_exact(hf,n,20,stage_deadline,rng)
    return h,c
```
Maybe final pair after CD2 and before flip. Good. If stage remaining after d4 small, flip no CD.
Could include `pair_search` use_grad default true. Good.

`run`:
Need careful time fractions. `budget_s` maybe float. Use B = float(budget_s). If B small, fractions okay. Deadline = t0+B-5. If B<20, just project parent and return? Add.
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    B=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t0+B-5.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=1e100; best_n=0
    try:
        parent=np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0=int(parent.size)
    except Exception:
        parent=None; n0=0
    if parent is None or n0 < 2:
        n0=600
        parent=make_random_balanced(n0,rng)
    if B < 60.0:
        h=project_feasible(parent,n0); c=compute_c5(h,n0)
        return h,c,n0
    tp=t0+0.35*B
    h,c=optimize_candidate(parent,n0,tp,rng,cd1=160,pair1=1500,cd2=90,pair2=900)
    if c < best_c:
        best_c=c; best_h=h; best_n=n0
    # resolutions
    res_list=[(800,0.52),(1000,0.66),(1200,0.78),(500,0.86)]
    for tn,frac in res_list:
        if best_h is None: break
        stage_deadline=min(deadline, t0+frac*B)
        if stage_deadline <= time.time()+5.0: continue
        if tn == best_n: continue
        x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,best_n,endpoint=False)
        x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,tn,endpoint=False)
        h_new=project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,best_h),tn)
        h,c=optimize_candidate(h_new,tn,stage_deadline,rng,cd1=80,pair1=700,cd2=50,pair2=450)
        if c < best_c - 1e-12 or (abs(c-best_c)<=1e-12 and tn < best_n):
            best_c=c; best_h=h; best_n=tn
    # alt seeds
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 60.0:
        alt_n = best_n if 400 <= best_n <= 1400 else (800 if best_n > 1400 else 600)
        seed_specs=[(0,0.91),(1,0.94),(2,0.97)]
        for typ,frac in seed_specs:
            stage_deadline=min(deadline, t0+frac*B)
            if stage_deadline <= time.time()+5.0: continue
            if typ == 0:
                hs=make_thue(alt_n)
            elif typ == 1:
                hs=make_two(alt_n, max(1, alt_n//5))
            else:
                hs=make_random_balanced(alt_n, rng)
            h,c=optimize_candidate(hs,alt_n,stage_deadline,rng,cd1=50,pair1=400,cd2=35,pair2=250)
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c=c; best_h=h; best_n=alt_n
    # final symmetry search
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
        h,c=try_rolls(best_h,best_n,rng,deadline-30.0,4)
        if c < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c=c; best_h=h; best_n=best_n
    # final polish
    if best_h is not None:
        t1=deadline-25.0
        if time.time() < t1:
            h,c=pair_search(best_h,best_n,t1,rng,max_iters=1000)
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c=c; best_h=h
        t2=deadline-5.0
        if time.time() < t2:
            h,c=coord_cd_exact(h,best_n,120,t2,rng)
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c=c; best_h=h
        # one last try rolls maybe
        if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
            h,c=try_rolls(best_h,best_n,rng,deadline-1.0,2)
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c=c; best_h=h
    if best_h is None:
        best_h=project_feasible(parent,n0); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n0); best_n=n0
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

```
def grad_lag(h,n,m):
    t=n-1; p=np.arange(n); g=np.zeros(n); j=p+m-t; m1=(j>=0)&(j<n); g[p[m1]]+=1-h[j[m1]]; i=p+t-m; m2=(i>=0)&(i<n); g[p[m2]]-=h[i[m2]]; return g
```

```
def add_nonaff(nonaff, loc, q, M):
    if 0 <= loc < M:
        for e,_ in nonaff:
            if e == loc: return nonaff
        nonaff.append((loc,q))
    return nonaff
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0.0:
            if float(h.max()) + diff <= 1.0 + 1e-14:
                h = h + diff
            else:
                lo, hi = 0.0, 1.0 - float(h.min())
                for _ in range(35):
                    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
                    s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
                    if s < target:
                        lo = mid
                    else:
                        hi = mid
                h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            if float(h.min()) + diff >= -1e-14:
                h = h + diff
            else:
                lo, hi = -float(h.max()), 0.0
                for _ in range(35):
                    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
                    s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
                    if s < target:
                        lo = mid
                    else:
                        hi = mid
                h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    res = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(res) > 1e-13:
        for _ in range(n):
            res = target - float(h.sum())
            if abs(res) <= 1e-14:
                break
            if res > 0.0:
                idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
                add = min(res, 1.0 - h[idx])
                if add <= 1e-18:
                    break
                h[idx] += add
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmin(h))
                sub = min(-res, h[idx])
                if sub <= 1e-18:
                    break
                h[idx] -= sub
    return h

def quadratic_feasible(q, c, b, V, lo, hi):
    if lo > hi:
        return []
    if q > 0.0:
        disc = b * b - 4.0 * q * (c - V)
        if disc < -1e-12:
            return []
        if disc <= 1e-12:
            rr = -b / (2.0 * q)
            if rr < lo - 1e-12 or rr > hi + 1e-12:
                return []
            rr = float(np.clip(rr, lo, hi))
            return [(rr, rr)]
        sq = float(np.sqrt(disc))
        r1 = (-b - sq) / (2.0 * q)
        r2 = (-b + sq) / (2.0 * q)
        l = lo if lo > r1 else r1
        u = hi if hi < r2 else r2
        if u - l > 1e-13:
            return [(l, u)]
        if u >= lo and l <= hi:
            return [(0.5 * (l + u), 0.5 * (l + u))]
        return []
    k = -q
    disc = b * b - 4.0 * k * (V - c)
    if disc <= 1e-12:
        return [(lo, hi)]
    sq = float(np.sqrt(disc))
    r1 = (-b - sq) / (2.0 * k)
    r2 = (-b + sq) / (2.0 * k)
    if hi <= r1 + 1e-12 or lo >= r2 - 1e-12:
        return [(lo, hi)]
    out = []
    if lo < r1:
        nu = hi if hi < r1 else r1
        if nu - lo > 1e-13:
            out.append((lo, nu))
        else:
            out.append((lo, lo))
    if hi > r2:
        nl = lo if r2 < lo else r2
        if hi - nl > 1e-13:
            out.append((nl, hi))
        else:
            out.append((hi, hi))
    return out

def intervals_for_V(V, cp, ap, cn, an, cz, data, lo_d, hi_d):
    L, U = lo_d, hi_d
    if ap.size:
        Lb = float(np.min((V - cp) / ap))
        if Lb < U:
            U = Lb
    if an.size:
        Ub = float(np.max((V - cn) / an))
        if Ub > L:
            L = Ub
    if cz > V + 1e-11:
        return []
    if L < lo_d:
        L = lo_d
    if U > hi_d:
        U = hi_d
    if L > U + 1e-12:
        return []
    iv = [(L, U)]
    for q, c, b in data:
        nv = []
        for l, u in iv:
            nv.extend(quadratic_feasible(q, c, b, V, l, u))
        iv = nv
        if not iv:
            return []
    out = []
    for l, u in iv:
        if l < lo_d:
            l = lo_d
        if u > hi_d:
            u = hi_d
        if u - l > 1e-13:
            out.append((l, u))
        else:
            out.append((0.5 * (l + u), 0.5 * (l + u)))
    return out

def prepare_constraint(aff, coeff, nonaff):
    if nonaff:
        mask = np.ones(aff.shape, dtype=bool)
        for loc, q in nonaff:
            mask[int(loc)] = False
        cl, al = aff[mask].copy(), coeff[mask].copy()
    else:
        cl, al = aff.copy(), coeff.copy()
    pos = al > 1e-12
    neg = al < -1e-12
    zero = ~(pos | neg)
    cp = cl[pos] if pos.any() else np.empty(0)
    ap = al[pos] if pos.any() else np.empty(0)
    cn = cl[neg] if neg.any() else np.empty(0)
    an = al[neg] if neg.any() else np.empty(0)
    cz = float(np.max(cl[zero])) if zero.any() else -1e300
    data = []
    for loc, q in nonaff:
        loc = int(loc)
        data.append((float(q), float(aff[loc]), float(coeff[loc])))
    return cp, ap, cn, an, cz, data

def add_nonaff(lst, loc, q, M):
    if 0 <= loc < M:
        f = False
        for e, _ in lst:
            if e == loc:
                f = True
                break
        if not f:
            lst.append((loc, q))
    return lst

def update_variable(h, c, idx, n, tau, bis, apply=True):
    h0 = float(h[idx])
    if h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-13:
        return 0.0, 0.0, False
    lo = tau - idx if tau - idx < idx else idx
    hi = tau + n - 1 - idx
    rb = idx + tau
    if rb > hi:
        hi = rb
    total = 2 * n - 1
    unaff = 0.0
    if lo > 0:
        m = float(np.max(c[:lo]))
        if m > unaff:
            unaff = m
    if hi + 1 < total:
        m = float(np.max(c[hi + 1:]))
        if m > unaff:
            unaff = m
    ms = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
    aff = c[ms]
    cur = max(float(np.max(aff)), unaff)
    if cur - unaff <= 1e-12:
        return 0.0, cur, False
    coeff = np.zeros(ms.shape, dtype=float)
    jf = idx + ms - tau
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    if vf.any():
        coeff[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    jb = idx - ms + tau
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    if vb.any():
        coeff[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    nonaff = []
    if lo <= tau <= hi:
        nonaff.append((tau - lo, -1.0))
    cp, ap, cn, an, cz, data = prepare_constraint(aff, coeff, nonaff)
    los, hij = -h0, 1.0 - h0
    if los > hij + 1e-13:
        return 0.0, cur, False
    low, high = unaff, cur
    if high - low <= 1e-13:
        return 0.0, cur, False
    for _ in range(bis):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        if intervals_for_V(mid, cp, ap, cn, an, cz, data, los, hij):
            high = mid
        else:
            low = mid
    iv = intervals_for_V(high, cp, ap, cn, an, cz, data, los, hij)
    if not iv:
        return 0.0, cur, False
    bd, bv = 0.0, cur
    for l, u in iv:
        for dd in (l, u, 0.5 * (l + u)):
            if dd < los - 1e-12 or dd > hij + 1e-12:
                continue
            dd = float(np.clip(dd, los, hij))
            vals = aff + coeff * dd
            for loc, q in nonaff:
                vals[int(loc)] += q * dd * dd
            val = float(np.max(vals))
            if unaff > val:
                val = unaff
            if val < bv - 1e-13:
                bv, bd = val, dd
    if bv < cur - 1e-12 and abs(bd) > 1e-13:
        nh = h0 + bd
        if 0.0 <= nh <= 1.0:
            if apply:
                upd = coeff * bd
                for loc, q in nonaff:
                    upd[int(loc)] += q * bd * bd
                c[lo:hi + 1] += upd
                np.maximum(c[lo:hi + 1], 0.0, out=c[lo:hi + 1])
            return bd, bv, True
    return 0.0, cur, False

def update_pair(h, c, i, j, n, tau, bis, apply=True):
    if i == j:
        return 0.0, 0.0, False
    lo_i = tau - i if tau - i < i else i
    hi_i = tau + n - 1 - i
    rb = i + tau
    if rb > hi_i:
        hi_i = rb
    lo_j = tau - j if tau - j < j else j
    hi_j = tau + n - 1 - j
    rb = j + tau
    if rb > hi_j:
        hi_j = rb
    lo = lo_i if lo_i < lo_j else lo_j
    hi = hi_i if hi_i > hi_j else hi_j
    total = 2 * n - 1
    unaff = 0.0
    if lo > 0:
        m = float(np.max(c[:lo]))
        if m > unaff:
            unaff = m
    if hi + 1 < total:
        m = float(np.max(c[hi + 1:]))
        if m > unaff:
            unaff = m
    ms = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
    M = ms.size
    aff = c[ms]
    cur = max(float(np.max(aff)), unaff)
    if cur - unaff <= 1e-12:
        return 0.0, cur, False
    coeff = np.zeros(M, dtype=float)
    jf = i + ms - tau
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    if vf.any():
        coeff[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    jb = i - ms + tau
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    if vb.any():
        coeff[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    jf = j + ms - tau
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    if vf.any():
        coeff[vf] -= 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    jb = j - ms + tau
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    if vb.any():
        coeff[vb] += h[jb[vb]]
    nonaff = [(tau - lo, -2.0)]
    add_nonaff(nonaff, (tau + (j - i)) - lo, 1.0, M)
    add_nonaff(nonaff, (tau + (i - j)) - lo, 1.0, M)
    cp, ap, cn, an, cz, data = prepare_constraint(aff, coeff, nonaff)
    hi0, hj0 = float(h[i]), float(h[j])
    los = max(-hi0, hj0 - 1.0)
    hij = min(1.0 - hi0, hj0)
    if los > hij + 1e-13:
        return 0.0, cur, False
    low, high = unaff, cur
    if high - low <= 1e-13:
        return 0.0, cur, False
    for _ in range(bis):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        if intervals_for_V(mid, cp, ap, cn, an, cz, data, los, hij):
            high = mid
        else:
            low = mid
    iv = intervals_for_V(high, cp, ap, cn, an, cz, data, los, hij)
    if not iv:
        return 0.0, cur, False
    bd, bv = 0.0, cur
    for l, u in iv:
        for dd in (l, u, 0.5 * (l + u)):
            if dd < los - 1e-12 or dd > hij + 1e-12:
                continue
            dd = float(np.clip(dd, los, hij))
            vals = aff + coeff * dd
            for loc, q in nonaff:
                vals[int(loc)] += q * dd * dd
            val = float(np.max(vals))
            if unaff > val:
                val = unaff
            if val < bv - 1e-13:
                bv, bd = val, dd
    if bv < cur - 1e-12 and abs(bd) > 1e-13:
        nhi, nhj = hi0 + bd, hj0 - bd
        if 0.0 <= nhi <= 1.0 and 0.0 <= nhj <= 1.0:
            if apply:
                upd = coeff * bd
                for loc, q in nonaff:
                    upd[int(loc)] += q * bd * bd
                c[lo:hi + 1] += upd
                np.maximum(c[lo:hi + 1], 0.0, out=c[lo:hi + 1])
            return bd, bv, True
    return 0.0, cur, False

def coord_cd_exact(h0, n, max_passes, deadline, rng, bis=None):
    if n < 4:
        h = project_feasible(h0, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h, n)
    if bis is None:
        bis = 24 if n <= 700 else (20 if n <= 1100 else 16)
    best_h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    tau = n - 1
    no = 0
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        h = best_h.copy()
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        updated = False
        order = rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
        for idx in order:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            d, val, ok = update_variable(h, c, int(idx), n, tau, bis, True)
            if ok:
                h[int(idx)] = float(np.clip(h[int(idx)] + d, 0.0, 1.0))
                updated = True
        if not updated:
            break
        hp = project_feasible(h, n)
        cp = compute_c5(hp, n)
        if cp < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_c, no = hp, cp, 0
        else:
            no += 1
            if no >= 6:
                break
    return best_h, best_c

def grad_lag(h, n, m):
    t = n - 1
    p = np.arange(n)
    g = np.zeros(n)
    j = p + m - t
    vf = (j >= 0) & (j < n)
    if vf.any():
        g[p[vf]] += 1.0 - h[j[vf]]
    i = p + t - m
    vb = (i >= 0) & (i < n)
    if vb.any():
        g[p[vb]] -= h[i[vb]]
    return g

def amin(g, m):
    return -1 if not m.any() else int(np.argmin(np.where(m, g, 1e300)))

def amax(g, m):
    return -1 if not m.any() else int(np.argmax(np.where(m, g, -1e300)))

def pair_search(h0, n, deadline, rng, max_iters=2000, bis=None, use_grad=True):
    if n < 4:
        h = project_feasible(h0, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h, n)
    if bis is None:
        bis = 22 if n <= 700 else (18 if n <= 1100 else 15)
    h = project_feasible(h0, n).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    tau = n - 1
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_count = float(c.max())
    best_c = best_count * (2.0 / n)
    stall = 0
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        i = j = -1
        if use_grad and (it % 4 == 0 or stall > 20):
            m = int(np.argmax(c))
            g = grad_lag(h, n, m)
            inc, dec = h < 1.0 - 1e-9, h > 1e-9
            if inc.any() and dec.any():
                i, j = amin(g, inc), amax(g, dec)
                if i == -1 or j == -1 or i == j:
                    i, j = amax(g, inc), amin(g, dec)
        if i == -1 or j == -1 or i == j:
            i = int(rng.randint(n))
            j = int(rng.randint(n - 1))
            if j >= i:
                j += 1
            if j >= n:
                j -= n
        d, val, ok = update_pair(h, c, i, j, n, tau, bis, False)
        if ok and val < best_count - 1e-10:
            d2, val2, ok2 = update_pair(h, c, i, j, n, tau, bis, True)
            if ok2:
                h[i] = float(np.clip(h[i] + d2, 0.0, 1.0))
                h[j] = float(np.clip(h[j] - d2, 0.0, 1.0))
                if val2 < best_count - 1e-12:
                    best_count = val2
                    best_c = val2 * (2.0 / n)
                    best_h = h.copy()
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 400:
                break
    hp = project_feasible(h, n)
    cp = compute_c5(hp, n)
    if cp < best_c - 1e-13:
        return hp, cp
    return best_h, best_c

def make_thue(n):
    t = np.array([0.0])
    while t.size < n:
        t = np.concatenate((t, 1.0 - t))
    return t[:n]

def make_random_balanced(n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n)
    k = n // 2
    if k:
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    if n % 2:
        h[int(rng.randint(n))] += 0.5
    return project_feasible(h, n)

def make_two(n, a=None):
    h = np.zeros(n)
    half = n // 2
    if a is None or a <= 0 or a > half:
        a = half // 2
    b = half - a
    h[:a] = 1.0
    if b:
        h[a:a + b] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h, n)

def try_rolls(h0, n, rng, deadline, extra=4):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    bases = (h, h[::-1], 1.0 - h, 1.0 - h[::-1])
    shifts = (0, n // 4, n // 2, n // 3, 2 * n // 5, 3 * n // 7)
    for b in bases:
        for s in shifts:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                return best_h, best_c
            hs = np.roll(b, int(s) % n)
            cs = compute_c5(hs, n)
            if cs < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_c, best_h = cs, hs.copy()
    if rng is not None:
        for _ in range(extra):
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            s = int(rng.randint(n))
            hs = np.roll(best_h, s)
            cs = compute_c5(hs, n)
            if cs < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_c, best_h = cs, hs.copy()
    return best_h, best_c

def optimize_candidate(h0, n, stage_deadline, rng, cd1=100, pair1=600, cd2=60, pair2=400):
    st = time.time()
    slen = max(1.0, stage_deadline - st)
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c = compute_c5(h, n)
    if slen < 30.0:
        return h, c
    d1 = st + 0.35 * slen
    h, c = coord_cd_exact(h, n, cd1, d1, rng)
    d2 = st + 0.60 * slen
    if time.time() < d2:
        h, c = pair_search(h, n, d2, rng, max_iters=pair1)
    d3 = st + 0.80 * slen
    h, c = coord_cd_exact(h, n, cd2, d3, rng)
    d4 = st + 0.90 * slen
    if time.time() < d4:
        h, c = pair_search(h, n, d4, rng, max_iters=pair2)
    hf = project_feasible(1.0 - h, n)
    cf = compute_c5(hf, n)
    if cf < c - 1e-12 and time.time() < stage_deadline - 5.0:
        h, c = coord_cd_exact(hf, n, 20, stage_deadline, rng)
    return h, c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    B = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + B - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e100
    best_n = 0
    try:
        parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(parent.size)
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        n0 = 0
    if parent is None or n0 < 2:
        n0 = 600
        parent = make_random_balanced(n0, rng)
    if B < 60.0:
        h = project_feasible(parent, n0)
        return h, compute_c5(h, n0), n0
    tp = t0 + 0.35 * B
    h, c = optimize_candidate(parent, n0, tp, rng, 160, 1500, 90, 900)
    if c < best_c:
        best_c, best_h, best_n = c, h, n0
    res_list = ((800, 0.52), (1000, 0.66), (1200, 0.78), (500, 0.86))
    for tn, frac in res_list:
        if best_h is None:
            break
        sd = min(deadline, t0 + frac * B)
        if sd <= time.time() + 5.0 or tn == best_n:
            continue
        x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, best_n, endpoint=False)
        x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, tn, endpoint=False)
        h_new = project_feasible(np.interp(x1, x0, best_h), tn)
        h, c = optimize_candidate(h_new, tn, sd, rng, 80, 700, 50, 450)
        if c < best_c - 1e-12 or (abs(c - best_c) <= 1e-12 and tn < best_n):
            best_c, best_h, best_n = c, h, tn
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 60.0:
        alt_n = best_n if 400 <= best_n <= 1400 else (800 if best_n > 1400 else 600)
        seed_specs = ((0, 0.91), (1, 0.94), (2, 0.97))
        for typ, frac in seed_specs:
            sd = min(deadline, t0 + frac * B)
            if sd <= time.time() + 5.0:
                continue
            if typ == 0:
                hs = make_thue(alt_n)
            elif typ == 1:
                hs = make_two(alt_n, max(1, alt_n // 5))
            else:
                hs = make_random_balanced(alt_n, rng)
            h, c = optimize_candidate(hs, alt_n, sd, rng, 50, 400, 35, 250)
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c, best_h, best_n = c, h, alt_n
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
        h, c = try_rolls(best_h, best_n, rng, deadline - 30.0, 4)
        if c < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c, best_h = c, h
    if best_h is not None:
        t1 = deadline - 25.0
        if time.time() < t1:
            h, c = pair_search(best_h, best_n, t1, rng, 1000)
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c, best_h = c, h
        t2 = deadline - 5.0
        if time.time() < t2:
            h, c = coord_cd_exact(best_h, best_n, 120, t2, rng)
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c, best_h = c, h
        if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
            h, c = try_rolls(best_h, best_n, rng, deadline - 1.0, 2)
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c, best_h = c, h
    if best_h is None:
        best_h = project_feasible(parent, n0)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n0)
        best_n = n0
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, best_n
```
